CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [97] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了HarnessOpt-Bench基准，用于评估LLM在Agent系统Harness优化方面的能力，发现优化器模型差异显著且优化空间巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛部署于Agent系统中，其性能不仅取决于模型权重，还严重依赖于Harness（包括提示词、工具、控制流、内存和编排代码等）。自动化Harness优化（通过AI系统迭代改进Harness）既是提升AI系统的重要途径，也是AI系统本身应具备的关键能力，但社区缺乏衡量前沿LLM在此任务上表现的统一协议。

Method: 提出HarnessOpt-Bench基准，让优化器LLM接收目标Agent的种子Harness、分级评估反馈和固定评估预算，通过编辑Harness并提名最终候选。使用不可见的保留测试集上的标准化增益评分，可信执行环境强制评估边界、计量资源使用并保存候选版本供审计。

Result: 在111次评分运行中评估了5个前沿LLM优化器，涵盖共享编码Harness和原生Harness，横跨4个下游任务。结果表明：优化器模型之间的差异大于其作用的编码Harness差异；原生Harness并不始终优于共享Harness；增益在任务和种子配置间变化显著。

Conclusion: 建立了Harness优化作为可测量且具有判别力的能力，当前表现存在巨大改进空间，为未来研究提供了明确的评估框架。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对财务报表等结构化长文档，传统分块嵌入检索方法存在结构性缺陷，提出的READ方法通过确定性操作实现无嵌入检索，在51个验证问题上准确率达58.8%，显著超越稠密检索的15.7%，与BM25无统计差异，表明问题在于嵌入而非智能体或词法检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档检索增强生成采用“分块-嵌入-取top-k近邻”的范式，但对于财务报表、审计报告、监管申报等重要文档类别，该设计存在结构性缺陷：86.8%内容为表格行，大量近乎相同的数值在同一嵌入空间竞争，数值的单位（如lakh或crore）中位数位于其上方13行处的表头，分块边界经常导致数值与单位分离，产生两个数量级的错误。

Method: 提出READ（可靠无嵌入智能体文档搜索）方法：智能体通过模型上下文协议（MCP）对原始文档执行三种确定性操作——归一化词法搜索、结构导航、有界跨度读取；轨迹是可重放的审计轨迹而非不透明的相似度分数。同时构建了表格感知分块器作为对照组。

Result: 在一份780页政府财务报告的51个验证问题上：READ准确率58.8%，稠密检索15.7%（p_Holm=2×10⁻⁵）；调优后稠密检索达35.3%，READ仍领先23.5个百分点（p_Holm=0.017）。使用相同循环但配top-k工具的智能体仅达27.5%。BM25与READ无统计差异。表格感知分块器在所有尝试的分块大小下仍有27-30%的数值分块缺少财年表头。

Conclusion: READ显著优于嵌入检索，收益来源于接口设计而非迭代过程；BM25与READ无统计差异，说明嵌入方法本身存在缺陷；研究区分了“嵌入与非嵌入检索”的差异，而非“智能体与词法搜索”的差异。对于结构化财务文档，无嵌入的确定性检索是更可靠的选择。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [99] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrajDebug框架用于LLM智能体系统的关键错误检测，并构建了包含486个标注失败轨迹的TrajErrBench基准，实验表明该方法优于现有基线并能为改进智能体提供可操作反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂任务中虽然表现优异，但面临级联错误和调试困难的问题。关键错误检测旨在定位导致最终失败的最早错误步骤，但现有研究面临两大挑战：（1）长轨迹中证据分散，难以识别单个错误；（2）失败轨迹常包含多个局部错误，需区分哪些真正导致最终失败。

Method: 提出TrajDebug错误生命周期追踪框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）多粒度历史压缩与基于证据的错误识别，解决长轨迹错误发现问题；（2）通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和终端影响，实现关键错误归因。同时构建TrajErrBench基准，包含486个来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的手动标注失败轨迹，涵盖工具使用和编程场景。

Result: 在多种智能体基准测试上的实验表明，TrajDebug在整体性能上优于现有基线方法。应用研究进一步证明，该方法提供的诊断能够为改进下游智能体成功率提供可操作的反馈。

Conclusion: TrajDebug有效解决了LLM智能体系统中的关键错误检测问题，其诊断结果能够指导智能体系统优化，作者将公开代码和数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [100] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了Nimblemind多智能体系统(nMAS)，实现了心力衰竭EHR数据的自动化特征工程，显著提升了模型性能(HFrEF AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910)，同时保证了特征的可追溯性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录(EHR)特征工程是临床研究和AI应用的主要瓶颈，占据数据科学家39-45%的工作量。心力衰竭作为影响约670万美国成年人的疾病，需要整合碎片化的EHR数据与疾病特异性的指南驱动临床推理，现有基于规则和大语言模型的方法仅能实现部分自动化，且可维护性和证据可追溯性有限。

Method: 开发了Nimblemind多智能体系统(nMAS)，这是一个基于证据链接和评分标准引导的自动化心力衰竭特征工程流程。系统在来自9个EHR源表的500个虚拟患者记录上进行评估，通过受限LLM进行审计，验证了结构完整性、评分标准合规性和来源可追溯性。

Result: nMAS生成了132个结构化特征和70个评分标准评分的聚合特征。添加聚合特征后，HFrEF表型分类的AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910。独立LLM评分标准评估显示，证据支持和方法合理性得分为最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了复杂心血管EHR数据自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性，但评估仅限于单机构队列，仍需外部验证来进一步确认系统的普适性和可靠性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


### [101] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了解释性人工智能（XAI）在评估方面的局限性，通过DetoxAI图像识别系统案例研究，提出了针对概念漂移的解释方法适应性，并讨论了数据、模型和解释协同演化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前解释性人工智能领域存在评估不足的核心问题，缺乏有效的方法来验证解释的质量和有效性，特别是在面对动态变化的数据流和概念漂移时，现有解释方法的适用性受到限制。

Method: 1）通过DetoxAI图像识别系统案例展示偏见检测和概念遗忘中的评估问题；2）实施基于人类认知的图像分类解释方法评估；3）开发适应概念漂移的数据流解释方法，重点关注反事实解释的适应性改进。

Result: 通过案例研究揭示了XAI评估的关键缺陷，证明了基于人类认知评估方法的有效性，展示了反事实解释在概念漂移场景下的适应性改进方案，为解决数据-模型-解释协同演化问题提供了实践经验和解决方案。

Conclusion: XAI领域需要建立更严格的评估框架，特别是针对动态环境下的解释适应性研究，未来应重点关注数据、模型和解释三者之间的协同演化机制，以提升可解释AI系统的可靠性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [102] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于可执行事件轨迹的视频事件计数评估方法，揭示了模型在时间推理中的阶段性失败模式：事件表示类型决定模型能否访问证据，高频高计数场景下性能急剧下降，增加采样率仅提升表面准确率而无法实现真实的事件恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实视频基准测试纠缠了事件数量、频率、持续时间和视觉复杂度，难以分离失败模式；程序化基准测试仅评分最终答案而不审计报告事件与可执行真值的一致性，缺乏细粒度的诊断能力。

Method: 提出轨迹引导的参数化分析框架，在弹球墙壁碰撞、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换三个受控视频任务中进行事件计数评估。生成2,190个视频，变化事件数量N和频率F，每个视频包含可执行事件轨迹用于能力面估计和时间戳级评估。

Result: 在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash对持续状态转换可可靠计数至12个事件（0.5-1.0 Hz），对瞬态眨眼事件无可靠正计数区域；高频高计数场景仅0.2%最终计数正确，仅恢复18.1%真实事件；提高采样率使弹球准确率从19.6%升至29.3%，但与真值序列一致性仅3.7%。

Conclusion: 轨迹引导分析将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转向细粒度诊断，揭示了时间推理失败的根源：事件表示类型决定证据访问能力，额外帧可能虚高最终分数而无真实事件恢复，提示策略改善有限，真实视频评估呈现相同的低事件计数成功集中现象。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [F$^2$Agent: Financial Fusion of Agentic Intelligence for Multimodal Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05668)
*Changshuo Liu,Yanzheng Jin,Shangfeng Cai,Peng Fang,Xiaokui Xiao,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了F²Agent，一种基于智能体融合的新型多模态金融交易框架，通过层次化智能体提取信号、模态感知自适应融合机制和噪声鲁棒一致性正则化，在六种资产上相比16个基线方法平均年化收益提升超过20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着金融信息来源日益多样化，多模态数据的有效利用对高质量交易至关重要。然而，现有基于LLM的方法在捕捉细粒度跨模态依赖方面存在不足，且容易受市场噪声影响，主要原因包括有限的多模态建模能力、不有效的融合机制以及鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出F²Agent框架：1)部署层次化专业智能体全面提取模态特定信号；2)引入模态感知自适应融合机制动态捕捉细粒度跨模态依赖；3)设计噪声鲁棒一致性正则化生成抗噪交易信号。

Result: 在6种股票和加密货币资产上的实验表明，F²Agent在多个交易指标上持续优于16个竞争基线，年化收益平均相对提升超过20%。具体而言，GOOG收益达120.48%，TSLA收益达148.41%，证明了其在不同市场动态下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: F²Agent成功解决了现有方法在多模态建模、融合机制和鲁棒性方面的局限性，通过智能体融合范式实现了对跨模态依赖的有效建模和噪声抑制，为金融交易领域提供了一个高效且鲁棒的多模态解决方案。

Abstract: With increasingly diverse and heterogeneous information sources, effectively leveraging multimodal data is becoming pivotal for high-quality financial trading. Although recent advancements in Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents have enabled the ingestion of multimodal inputs, existing methods fail to capture nuanced cross-modal dependencies and remain vulnerable to market noise, due to limited multimodal modeling, ineffective fusion mechanisms, and inadequate robustness. To address these challenges, we propose F$^2$Agent, a novel multimodal agentic paradigm driven by the Financial Fusion of Agentic Intelligence. F$^2$Agent first deploys a hierarchy of specialized agents to comprehensively extract modality-specific signals. It further introduces a modality-aware adaptive fusion mechanism coupled with noise-robust consistency regularization to dynamically capture fine-grained inter-modality dependencies and generate noise-resilient trading signals. Extensive experiments on six stocks and cryptocurrency assets demonstrate that F$^2$Agent consistently outperforms 16 competitive baselines across multiple trading metrics, with over 20% relative improvement in annualized return on average. Notably, F$^2$Agent delivers returns of 120.48% on GOOG and 148.41% on TSLA, demonstrating its efficacy and robustness in varying market dynamics.

</details>


### [104] [A Two-Tier Perspective on Inference-Time Parallelism in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05791)
*Zihan Xu,Haolin Tian,Hai Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了TIPEX框架，统一了多智能体系统推理时的两种并行级别（副本并行和结构并行），并在GAIA基准上验证了并行策略能显著提升准确率并降低延迟，但会增加token消耗，且不同复杂度的任务适合不同的并行策略组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型驱动的多智能体系统在推理时需要多次模型调用和复杂的协调机制，执行策略直接影响系统的准确率、延迟和计算成本。虽然并行执行能提升推理效率，但目前缺乏对副本并行和结构并行这两种不同层级并行方式的系统研究，缺少统一的组织和协调机制。

Method: 提出TIPEX框架，将多智能体系统中的并行性建模为两个决策层：副本并行（在任务层级探索多个完整解决方案路径）和结构并行（通过任务分解在单个解决方案路径内实现并发执行）。该框架在统一的执行语义下协调这两个层级的并行性，支持对不同并行策略和参数配置进行系统组合与分析。

Result: 在GAIA基准测试上的系统实验表明：推理时并行性可以显著提升准确率并降低端到端延迟，代价是token消耗增加；副本并行和结构并行在不同任务复杂度下呈现互补效应，中等难度任务最受益于二者的协调配合；过度激进的并行策略不一定会带来更好的性能。

Conclusion: 推理时并行性是提升多智能体系统性能的有效手段，但需要根据任务复杂度合理配置并行策略。TIPEX框架为系统研究和优化并行执行提供了统一的理论基础和实践工具，揭示了副本并行与结构并行之间的互补关系，为实际应用中的策略选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-driven multi-agent systems typically require multiple model invocations and complex coordination during inference, and their execution strategies directly affect system accuracy, latency, and computational cost. Parallel execution provides a means to improve inference-time efficiency. From the perspective of inference-time execution, this paper models parallelism in multi-agent systems as two distinct levels of decision processes: Replica Parallelism, which explores multiple complete solution paths at the task level, and Structural Parallelism, which enables concurrent execution within a single solution path through task decomposition. However, the roles of different forms of parallelism and their interrelationships still lack systematic study in terms of unified organization and coordination. We therefore propose TIPEX, a controllable execution framework that unifies these two levels of parallelism and coordinates their roles within the inference process under a unified execution semantics while supporting systematic combinations and analyses of different parallel strategies and parameter configurations. Systematic experiments on the GAIA benchmark demonstrate that inference-time parallelism can significantly improve accuracy and reduce end-to-end latency at the cost of increased token consumption. Further analysis shows that Replica and Structural Parallelism exhibit complementary effects across task complexities, with tasks of intermediate difficulty benefiting most from their coordination, while overly aggressive parallel strategies do not necessarily yield better performance.

</details>


### [105] [Certifying Collective Reasoning in Multi-Agent Systems via Koopman Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05956)
*Nuzhat Khan,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Koopman算子理论的框架，为LLM多智能体辩论系统提供了可证明的收敛保证、时间界限和决策可解释性，实现了对集体推理过程的可审计认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体集体辩论投票系统虽然能提高任务准确性，但在系统层面仍是黑盒：缺乏收敛性的原则性检验、无法界定所需轮次上限、且无法忠实记录决策驱动因素。这些限制阻碍了对集体智能行为的理解和信任。

Method: 将智能体集体视为通信图上的非线性动力系统，利用Koopman转移算子理论构建精确的线性表示。通过从交互轨迹估计Koopman算子并分析其谱特性，提取三个机器可验证证书：次主导特征值λ₂确定推理时间尺度并计算收敛截止时间，对应特征向量识别一致性派系，主导谱坐标形成可审计的消息基。

Result: 在注意力共识模型上，截止时间与观测收敛呈0.93的log-log相关性，在24种配置的96%情况下给出收敛界限；当谱认证亚稳态时归因精确；32个坐标中的8个以99.7%保真度保留决策；从15次辩论学习的证书在60/60次留出辩论上均有效。整个研究在CPU上几分钟内完成。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子的谱认证方法为集体推理系统提供了实用的可信任层，实现了收敛保证、时间界限和可解释性的理论统一，为LLM多智能体系统的可信部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: Orchestrated collectives of large language model (LLM) agents that debate and vote are an emerging form of computational intelligence: the intelligent behaviour resides in the \emph{interaction}, not in any single agent. They improve task accuracy, yet remain black boxes at the system level: there is no principled test of convergence, no bound on the rounds needed, and no faithful account of what drove a decision. This paper develops a novel framework based on Koopman operator theory and validates its theoretical guarantees on multi-agent consensus dynamics. Treating the collective as one nonlinear dynamical system on a communication graph, we read its essential behaviour off the spectrum of its Koopman transfer operator, an exact linear representation of the nonlinear dynamics estimated from interaction traces. The spectrum yields three machine-checkable certificates: the sub-dominant eigenvalue $λ_2$ fixes the intrinsic timescale of reasoning and yields a convergence deadline computable \emph{before} the debate runs; its eigenvector names the coherent factions the collective reasons in, and $|λ_2|$ certifies when that explanation is valid; and the leading spectral coordinates form a compressed, auditable message basis. On an attention-consensus model, the deadline tracks observed convergence with log--log correlation $0.93$ and bounds it in 96\% of 24 configurations; attribution is exact whenever the spectrum certifies metastability; eight of 32 coordinates preserve the decision at 99.7\% fidelity; and a certificate learned from 15 debates held on 60/60 held-out debates. The study runs in minutes on a CPU, making spectral certification a practical layer for trustworthy collective reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [ASGE-RR: Agentic Service Graph Embedding with Revisable Reservations for Dynamic AI-Agent Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06033)
*Trond Vatten,Yuming Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了ASGE-RR控制器，通过可修正的资源预留机制解决AI智能体服务图中的在线网络控制问题，在WAN测试床上相比现有方法提升了最多10%的工作流价值完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体工作流涉及跨网络的模型、存储和工具的远程调用，这些依赖调用在运行时才逐步揭示，形成智能体服务图(ASG)。传统资源分配方法可能消耗掉后续高价值工作流调用所需的容量，因此需要一种能够为可能的未来调用预留资源的同时满足约束的在线控制机制。

Method: 将问题建模为智能体服务图嵌入(ASGE)在线网络控制问题，提出ASGE-RR控制器，采用可修正的预留机制。控制器基于预测的工作流延续评估候选副本-路径映射，并在获取新执行信息时更新预留，为可能的未来调用保护容量，同时满足容量、成本和截止时间约束。

Result: 使用OpenHands和GPT Researcher工作流在Docker测试床和WAN测试床两种环境下进行评估。发现所有AI智能体任务都暴露至少一个可在连接建立前引导的运行时揭示依赖调用。ASGE-RR在WAN测试床上相比同信息滚动 horizon 控制器和当前调用引导控制器完成了最多10%更多的工作流价值。

Conclusion: 运行时揭示的工作流结构创造了新的网络控制机会：为可能的未来调用保护资源使更多AI智能体工作流能够按时完成。即使在中小规模实验环境中，ASGE-RR也已展现出明显潜力。

Abstract: AI-agent workflows often involve remote calls to models, memory stores, and tools distributed across a network. As execution progresses, these dependency calls collectively form an agentic service graph (ASG). Unlike traditional service requests, many dependency calls are revealed only at runtime. Consequently, allocating resources to a currently visible call may consume capacity later needed by a call from a higher-value workflow. We formulate this challenge as Agentic Service Graph Embedding (ASGE), an online network-control problem that maps runtime-revealed workflow calls to service replicas and network paths under capacity, cost and deadline constraints. We present ASGE-RR, an online ASGE controller with revisable reservations. ASGE-RR protects capacity for likely future calls while enforcing the constraints. ASGE-RR evaluates candidate replica-and-path mappings against predicted workflow continuations and updates reservations as new execution information becomes available.
  We evaluate ASGE-RR using OpenHands and GPT Researcher workflows executed with gpt-5.6-luna and replayed over in two complementary experimental environments, a controlled Docker testbed and a WAN testbed. The investigation shows that all the evaluated AI-agent tasks expose at least one runtime-revealed dependency call that can be steered before connection establishment. Exploiting this control point, even though the experimental environments are small-scale, ASGE-RR already demonstrates noticeable potential: It completes (up to) 10% more workflow value than a same-information rolling-horizon controller and a current-call steering controller on the WAN testbed. The results suggest that runtime-revealed workflow structure creates a new network control opportunity: protecting resources for likely future calls allows more AI-agent workflows to finish in time.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Multi-Agent Transformer for Queue-Level XR Traffic Scheduling in TSN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05340)
*Marcos Carvalho,Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Daniel F. Macedo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于多智能体变换器(MAT)的队列级XR流量调度方法，解决了MEC环境中异构XR应用的资源竞争问题，实现了显著的延迟和失败率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSN和MEC为XR等时间敏感应用提供超可靠低延迟通信，但XR的广泛应用带来了挑战：(1)MEC环境中共置服务导致共享网络资源竞争；(2)XR流量类型具有不同的时间要求特性和关键性，增加了环境复杂性；(3)现有强化学习方法采用集中式或高层多智能体设计，主要针对周期性可预测的工业流量，存在细粒度控制能力不足和对动态异构XR流量适应性差的问题。

Method: 提出队列级多智能体强化学习方法进行XR流量调度，采用多智能体变换器(MAT)通过对智能体观测和动作的注意力机制来建模队列间依赖关系，实现异构共置XR应用间的隐式协调。

Result: 仿真结果显示，所提方法优于基线方法，实现高达71.42%的延迟降低和83.2%的失败率降低，同时所有队列均保持高可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于MAT的队列级多智能体强化学习方法有效解决了动态异构XR流量调度问题，通过捕获队列间依赖关系实现了更好的性能表现，为TSN/MEC环境下的XR应用调度提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) and Mobile Edge Computing (MEC) hold strong potential for enabling ultra-reliable low-latency communication for time-sensitive applications, such as eXtended Reality (XR). However, the widespread adoption of XR introduces significant challenges due to co-located services in MEC environments, leading to contention for shared network resources. Moreover, XR traffic types have distinct characteristics and criticality in terms of timing requirements, further increasing the complexity and dynamics of such environments. Although reinforcement learning has shown promise for TSN scheduling optimization in dynamic network scenarios, existing approaches rely on centralized or high-level multi-agent designs and are typically tailored to periodic and predictable industrial traffic, limiting their applicability to XR workloads. As a result, these approaches suffer from (i) limited ability to capture inter-queue dependencies due to coarse-grained control, and (ii) poor adaptability to highly dynamic and heterogeneous XR traffic. To address these gaps, we propose a multi-agent reinforcement learning approach for queue-level XR traffic scheduling. We adopt the multi-agent transformer (MAT) to model inter-queue dependencies via attention over agents' observations and actions, enabling implicit coordination across heterogeneous co-located XR applications. Our simulation results show that the proposed method outperforms baselines, achieving up to 71.42% latency reduction and up to 83.2% reduction in failure rate, while consistently achieving high reliability across all queues.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Online Traffic Scheduling in Time-Sensitive Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05346)
*Marcos Carvalho,Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Daniel F. Macedo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体强化学习(MARL)的时间敏感网络(TSN)调度框架，使用HAPPO算法对多个TSN队列进行分布式协同优化，在动态MEC环境中实现了帧等待时间和最坏情况延迟的显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSN调度方案主要依赖静态优化技术或基于固定流量模式的集中式学习模型，在动态MEC环境中效果受限。实际MEC环境通常承载多个共置的XR流量流，其特性随时间演化并产生复杂的队列间依赖关系，而现有调度器无法有效捕获这些依赖关系，因此需要能够在变化流量条件下协调多个TSN队列的自适应、去中心化调度机制。

Method: 提出多智能体强化学习(MARL)框架用于TSN调度，将每个TSN队列建模为自治智能体。采用异构智能体近端策略优化(HAPPO)算法显式建模智能体间依赖关系，并跨队列联合优化服务交付，实现分布式协同调度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法将平均帧等待时间减少高达26.8%，最坏情况延迟降低约16.8%，在动态XR驱动的MEC场景中表现出显著有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于HAPPO的MARL框架能够有效解决动态MEC环境中多队列TSN调度问题，通过显式建模队列间依赖关系实现协同优化，为XR等延迟敏感应用提供了高效的分布式调度解决方案。

Abstract: Time-sensitive networking (TSN) is increasingly integrated into mobile edge computing (MEC) to support applications with stringent latency requirements, such as extended reality (XR). However, existing TSN scheduling solutions predominantly rely on static optimization techniques or centralized learning models that are based on fixed traffic patterns, limiting their effectiveness in dynamic environments. In practice, MEC environments often host multiple co-located XR traffic flows whose characteristics evolve over time, creating complex inter-queue dependencies that current schedulers fail to capture. Addressing these challenges requires adaptive, decentralized scheduling mechanisms capable of coordinating multiple TSN queues under varying traffic conditions. To this end, this paper proposes a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework for TSN scheduling, where each TSN queue is modeled as an autonomous agent. The Heterogeneous-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (HAPPO) algorithm is employed to explicitly model inter-agent dependencies and jointly optimize service delivery across queues. The simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces average frame waiting times by up to 26.8% and worst-case delays by approximately 16.8%, highlighting its effectiveness in dynamic XR-driven MEC scenarios.

</details>


### [109] [DTMC-Based Analysis and Scheduling for Periodic Flows with Proactive HARQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05639)
*Haozhe Yi,Junyi Liu,Maolin Yang,Haochun Liang,Bo Liu,Feng Hong,Chaowei Liu,Hongbiao Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间马尔可夫链的框架，用于分析周期性流量下的主动HARQ调度问题，通过扩展状态空间捕捉跨时隙定时效应，结合遗传算法提升系统可调度性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超可靠低时延通信（URLLC）需要为异构周期性流量提供严格的可靠性和时延保证。主动HARQ虽可通过早期终止提升资源效率，但时隙级定时效应（特别是反馈延迟）使可调度性分析变得复杂，现有研究对此缺乏系统建模。

Method: 提出一种针对周期性流的离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）框架，通过扩展状态空间显式刻画HARQ往返时延等跨时隙定时效应。该框架计算满足异构可靠性与时延约束所需的传输机会，并采用两阶段遗传算法实现基于偏移的调度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在工业级URLLC流量场景下，相比反应式HARQ、K-Repetition以及无保证的主动HARQ，本方法可显著提高可调度性，且计算开销在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 所提出的DTMC建模框架有效解决了主动HARQ中的跨时隙定时耦合问题，为周期性URLLC流量提供了可靠、高效且可计算的调度分析工具。

Abstract: Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC) requires strict reliability and latency guarantees for heterogeneous periodic traffic. Proactive HARQ improves resource efficiency through early termination, but slot-level timing effects, particularly delayed feedback, complicate schedulability analysis.
  This paper presents a discrete-time Markov chain (DTMC)-based framework for periodic flows with proactive HARQ. By expanding the state space, the model captures HARQ round-trip time and other cross-slot timing effects. The framework determines the transmission opportunities required to satisfy heterogeneous reliability and latency constraints and supports offset-based scheduling through a two-stage genetic algorithm.
  Simulations with industrial URLLC traffic show that the proposed method achieves higher schedulability than reactive HARQ, K-Repetition, and non-guaranteed proactive HARQ, with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [110] [Closed-Loop Decision-Focused Learning for User-Aware Cloud Orchestration under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05735)
*Dongbin Jiao,Xubo Zhang,Huakang Lin,Ke Shang,Shi Yan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CL-DFL框架，通过端到端闭环学习解决云编排中异构作业调度问题，在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间实现了更优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变云工作负载导致资源利用不均，现有预测-优化框架存在两阶段解耦问题，难以在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出CL-DFL框架，整合基于MTGNN的时空预测器和基于TPE的零阶决策聚焦学习机制，建立端到端反馈路径；开发GNeuro-PLS策略，将GRPO融入协作局部搜索以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，CL-DFL在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率方面取得更优权衡，能控制常规负载下的过载风险，并在高饱和场景下保持韧性。

Conclusion: CL-DFL框架有效解决了异构作业调度中的多目标组合优化问题，相比现有方法在多项性能指标上表现更优，为云编排提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Time-varying cloud workloads often cause resource under-utilization during off-peak periods and resource contention during peak periods. Existing prediction-then-optimization (PTO) frameworks suffer from two-stage decoupling, hindering the balance among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. We formulate heterogeneous job scheduling as a multi-objective combinatorial optimization problem (MOCOP) under uncertain constraints and propose a closed-loop decision-focused learning (CL-DFL) framework for cloud orchestration. CL-DFL integrates a Multivariate Time-series Graph Neural Network (MTGNN)-based spatio-temporal predictor with a zeroth-order decision-focused learning (DFL) mechanism based on the tree-structured Parzen estimator (TPE). This integration establishes an end-to-end (E2E) feedback pathway between resource perception and scheduling decisions. Furthermore, we develop the GNeuro-PLS strategy by incorporating group relative policy optimization (GRPO) into cooperative local search to improve robustness under heterogeneous workloads. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that CL-DFL achieves superior trade-offs among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. It effectively controls overload risks under regular workloads and maintains resilience under highly saturated scenarios compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [111] [5G ISAC-Based UAV Detection and 3-D Tracking Using Uplink Sounding Reference Signals on an End-to-End O-RAN Simulation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05826)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan,Shiva R. Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于O-RAN的5G ISAC端到端仿真测试平台，通过两种独立方案解决高度不可观测问题，实现了对低空无人机的检测与三维跟踪，高度估计RMSE达1.8米，且在10 Mbps上行通信负载下保持检测覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISAC系统中，单 bistatic 配对导致高度不可观测，跟踪器需要高度先验信息。本文旨在消除高度先验依赖，建立高度可观测性，验证ISAC系统在低空无人机检测与三维跟踪中的可行性。

Method: 1. 构建基于OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT的开源O-RAN仿真测试平台；2. 将NR上行探测参考信号(SRS)复用为被动雷达波形；3. 在gNB内部署PHY层感知阶段生成检测，通过自定义E2服务模型传递至扩展卡尔曼滤波跟踪xApp；4. 提出两种消除高度先验的方案：平面接收阵列提供垂直孔径，以及第二发射机提供距离分集；5. 进行离线射线追踪研究与实时实验验证。

Result: 1. 高度估计从故意错误的初始高度收敛至1.8米RMSE；2. 信号处理链和端到端实时栈均建立了高度可观测性；3. 在10 Mbps上行通信负载下保持检测覆盖；4. 无需修改NR标准或专用感知波形。

Conclusion: 通过平面接收阵列和第二发射机两种方案成功建立了高度可观测性，验证了基于O-RAN的5G ISAC系统在低空无人机检测与三维跟踪中的有效性，且在通信负载下保持感知性能，为ISAC系统的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) lets cellular infrastructure serve communication users and sense on the same waveform. We present an end-to-end O-RAN simulation testbed for 5G ISAC targeting low-altitude UAV detection and 3-D tracking, built from open-source components: OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC and Sionna RT, in which the NR Uplink Sounding Reference Signal is repurposed as a passive radar waveform: a PHY-layer sensing stage inside the gNB produces detections that reach an Extended Kalman Filter tracking xApp over a custom E2 service model, with no change to the NR standard and no dedicated sensing waveform. A single bistatic pair leaves elevation unobservable, so the tracker needs a height prior; we remove it two independent ways and measure both - a planar receive array supplying a vertical aperture, and a second transmitter supplying range diversity. Both live results corroborate an offline ray-traced study of the same estimator, which converges from a deliberately wrong initial altitude to 1.8 m RMSE at a consistent filter, so altitude observability is established both in the signal-processing chain and end to end through the live stack. Detection coverage is preserved under a concurrent 10 Mbps uplink communications load.

</details>


### [112] [BALANCE: Hybrid Autoregressive-Speculative LLM Inference in Wireless Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05926)
*Guanqiao Qu,Shuo Chen,Qian Chen,Kin K. Leung,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出BALANCE框架，通过混合自回归解码和推测解码，在边缘服务器上同时支持两种模式，解决LLM边缘推理中的延迟-内存权衡问题，显著提升任务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘LLM推理面临延迟-内存权衡挑战：自回归解码（AD）顺序生成导致长延迟，推测解码（SD）虽加速推理但增加内存开销。在有限边缘计算资源下，单一方法无法高效服务具有异构需求的用户。

Method: 提出混合自回归-推测推理框架（BALANCE），边缘服务器同时托管SLM和LLM，将用户分配至AD或SD模式并行执行。构建任务吞吐量最大化问题，联合优化用户调度和计算资源分配，并开发多项式时间算法将问题分解为两个子问题，获得具有常数近似保证的次优解。

Result: 实验表明BALANCE持续优于传统AD和SD方法，显著提升任务吞吐量。在用户延迟需求和服务器内存约束下，算法能够有效最大化服务用户数量。

Conclusion: BALANCE框架成功解决了边缘LLM推理中的延迟-内存权衡问题，通过混合解码策略和优化资源分配，实现了边缘计算资源的高效利用，为下一代移动网络中的边缘推理服务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Edge inference is a promising paradigm to provide large language model (LLM) inference services in next-generation mobile networks. LLM inference mainly relies on two approaches: Autoregressive decoding (AD) generates output tokens sequentially, resulting in long latency; Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates inference by using a small language model (SLM) to generate multiple draft tokens for LLM verification, but incurs extra memory costs. Due to this latency-memory tradeoff, neither approach alone can efficiently serve users with heterogeneous demands under limited edge computing resources. To address this challenge, we propose a hybrid autoregressive-speculative inference (BALANCE) framework for edge LLM inference. In BALANCE, an edge server hosts both an SLM and an LLM, assigns each user to AD or SD, and performs the two modes simultaneously. To maximize the number of served users, we formulate a task throughput maximization problem to jointly determine user scheduling and computing resource allocation between AD and SD under user latency requirements and server memory constraints. Since the problem is NP-hard, we develop a polynomial-time algorithm that transforms the original problem into two sub-problems and obtains a sub-optimal solution with a constant approximation guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that BALANCE consistently outperforms conventional AD and SD and significantly improves task throughput.

</details>


### [113] [ML-for-ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06046)
*Yutong Zhao,Noga H. Rotman,Gianni Antichi,Ran Ben Basat*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出ML-for-ML跨层优化框架，通过联合优化网络参数和机器学习参数，在共享云集群环境下将达到目标损失的时间缩短了42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI训练任务快速增长，在共享云集群中，训练任务与并发工作负载竞争网络资源。现有系统将网络机制与ML训练选择分开优化：网络控制字节传输，ML系统控制通信时机和规模，这种分离导致端到端性能无法达到最优。

Method: 提出ML-for-ML跨层优化视角，在统一的time-to-target-loss目标下，联合选择网络层参数和ML层参数（knobs），实现跨层协同优化而非分离优化。

Result: 初步原型实验表明，通过协同优化ML和网络参数，达到目标损失的速度提升高达42%。

Conclusion: 网络层和ML层的联合优化能够显著提升AI训练的端到端性能，证明了跨层协同设计在共享云环境中的重要价值。

Abstract: AI training workloads are growing rapidly, making their time, energy, and infrastructure costs increasingly important. In shared cloud clusters, training and fine-tuning jobs compete with co-running workloads for network resources, while network mechanisms and ML training choices are typically optimized separately: networking controls how bytes move, whereas ML systems control when and how much communication occurs. We argue that this separation leaves end-to-end performance on the table.
  We present ML-for-ML, a cross-layer perspective in which network-side and ML-side knobs are selected jointly under a shared time-to-target-loss objective. Our preliminary prototype shows that by co-optimizing the ML and network parameters, we reach the target loss up to 42% faster.

</details>


### [114] [MARS: Multipath Adaptive Reliable Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06101)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MARS是一种接收端驱动、转发器辅助的多路径传输方案，通过层级同步的覆盖路径发现和耦合的拥塞控制，在不改变IP路由的前提下实现了比纯端到端设计更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多路径传输方案存在实际权衡：端到端传输（如MPTCP和MPQUIC）易于部署但受限于端点可见路径和拥塞反馈延迟；而路由或转发器辅助的方法往往需要基础设施支持或缺乏安全的转发协调机制。

Method: 提出MARS，一种接收端驱动、转发器辅助的多路径传输方案。MARS结合了层级同步的覆盖路径发现和耦合的消费者/转发器拥塞控制，使其能够安全扩展可用的转发机会并在瓶颈处快速响应。MARS以可增量部署的UDP覆盖层形式运行，可部署在客户端、服务器、中继或CDN类节点。

Result: 在端点部署时，MARS与端到端多路径基线方案性能相当；在协作覆盖转发器部署下，MARS暴露了更丰富的可用路径多样性，相比ECMP受限基线减少最多81.5%的最大P95流完成时间。在相同路径集下，MARS实现了更低的P95 FCT和更强的丢包鲁棒性，同时能从瞬态链路故障中快速恢复。

Conclusion: ICN风格的接收端驱动转发可以作为WAN多路径的可部署覆盖传输基础，在不改变IP路由的前提下提供超越纯端到端设计的性能优势。

Abstract: Multipath transport is increasingly important for Internet/WAN services that move large data volumes across heterogeneous paths, including geo-distributed analytics, content distribution, and cloud-service pipelines. Existing solutions, however, face a practical trade-off: end-to-end transports such as MPTCP and MPQUIC are deployable but limited by endpoint-visible paths and delayed congestion feedback, while routing-or forwarder-assisted approaches often require infrastructure support or lack safe coordination across forwarding choices. This paper presents MARS, a receiver-driven, forwarder-assisted multipath transport for Internet/WAN environments. MARS combines tier-synchronized overlay path discovery with coupled consumer/forwarder congestion control, enabling it to safely expand usable forwarding opportunities and react near bottlenecks. It runs as an incrementally deployable UDP overlay at clients, servers, relays, or CDN-like nodes. We implement MARS in simulation and as a prototype, and evaluate it through large-scale simulation and Mininet emulation under different deployment scales, loss rates, and failure scenarios. The results show that MARS provides deployment-dependent benefits: with endpoint-only deployment, it remains competitive with end-to-end multipath baselines; with cooperating overlay forwarders, it exposes richer usable path diversity and reduces max p95 flow completion time by up to 81.5\% over ECMP-limited baselines. Even against path-expanded end-to-end baselines given the same path set, MARS achieves lower worst-case p95 FCT and stronger robustness under packet loss, while also recovering quickly from transient link failures. These results demonstrate that ICN-style receiver-driven forwarding can serve as a deployable overlay transport substrate for WAN multipath, providing benefits beyond purely end-to-end designs without requiring changes to IP routing.

</details>


### [115] [MultiMoQ: Multi-Access Media-Over-QUIC for Robust Immersive Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06102)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Ruonan Chai,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了MultiMoQ框架，基于MoQ实现多接入分块流媒体传输，在受限带宽条件下提升360度视频的传输鲁棒性和播放连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沉浸式视频流（如360度视频）在支持大规模并发用户的高码率传输方面存在困难：粗粒度传输限制了响应性，并发分块流缺乏协调支持；虽然Media over QUIC (MoQ)为大规模媒体分发提供了新方案，但在带宽受限条件下缺乏鲁棒性，易导致播放卡顿。

Method: 提出MultiMoQ，一个基于MoQ的多接入分块流框架。重新设计MoQ的传输机制以支持跨多接入路径的鲁棒高码率流传输，同时提供灵活的分块调度和无缝接入切换功能。实现了完整原型系统并在网络仿真环境下进行评估。

Result: 在异构真实网络条件下与DASH和标准MoQ对比，MultiMoQ提高了增强层分块和基础视频的吞吐量，降低了增强层分块的尾端到端延迟，同时保持音频连续性并避免了标准MoQ的持续卡顿问题；消融实验验证了多接入控制对播放连续性的贡献。

Conclusion: MultiMoQ有效解决了MoQ在带宽受限条件下的鲁棒性问题，实现了更平滑的视口播放，为大规模沉浸式视频流提供了一种可靠的传输方案。

Abstract: Live immersive video streaming, particularly 360-degree video, is increasingly adopted in applications such as virtual events, sports broadcasting, and remote education. Existing approaches struggle to support high-bitrate immersive streaming for large numbers of concurrent users, with coarse-grained delivery limiting responsiveness and insufficient support for coordinating concurrent tile streams. Media over QUIC (MoQ) has recently emerged as a promising solution for large-scale media delivery, yet it lacks robustness under bandwidth-constrained conditions, often resulting in playback stalls. To address these challenges, we present MultiMoQ, a multi-access tile streaming framework built on MoQ that redesigns its delivery mechanism for robust high-bitrate streaming across multiple access paths while supporting flexible tile scheduling and seamless access switching. We implement a fully functional prototype of MultiMoQ and evaluate it in network emulation under heterogeneous real-world network conditions, comparing against Dynamic Adaptive Streaming over HTTP (DASH) and standard MoQ. Results show that MultiMoQ increases goodput for enhancement tiles and base video and reduces enhancement-tile tail end-to-end latency relative to DASH, while preserving audio continuity and avoiding the persistent stalls of standard MoQ. These transport gains translate into smoother viewport playback, and the ablation results further confirm the contribution of multi-access control to playback continuity.

</details>


### [116] [From Passive Mirrors to Active Agents: Holonic Digital Twins for Physical AI over Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06227)
*Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Omar Hashash,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出HDT-Net框架，通过层级数字孪生实现物理AI的实时推理与协作，解决当前AI在物理系统中缺乏可靠世界模型的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI工具（深度学习、生成式AI）在物理系统（机器人、车辆）中失效，无法在不确定性下维持可靠的世界模型并进行长期规划，且现有无线网络架构仅优化吞吐量等指标，无法支持需要共享时空上下文的实时物理AI协调。

Method: 提出全息数字孪生网络（HDT-Net）框架：1）HDT采用跨越物理代理和网络边缘的分层结构；2）因果马尔可夫毯确定协调代理并支持多域干预的反事实推理；3）主动推断通过最小化期望自由能统一感知、行动与学习；4）范畴论确保异构代理间语义结构保持；5）整合信息理论量化集体智能涌现。

Result: 框架实现了物理AI的实时推理，使代理能够主动推理环境而非被动镜像物理资产，支持自主局部推理与邻居协作形成集体智能单元，并在异构代理间保持语义一致性。

Conclusion: HDT-Net为物理智能的无线网络编排提供了理论基础与实现架构，通过主动推断、范畴论和整合信息理论的融合，实现了从个体推理到集体智能的演进，为未来物理AI系统奠定了框架基础。

Abstract: Despite advances in artificial intelligence (AI) across multiple sectors, today's AI tools, including deep learning and generative AI, still fail when embedded into physical systems, such as robots and vehicles operating under real-world physical laws. This stems from their inability to maintain reliable world models for long-horizon planning under uncertainty and generalize to unseen scenarios. In this context, wireless networks, through pervasive sensing and communication, can orchestrate physical intelligence. However, current architectures optimize throughput, latency, and reliability and cannot support real-time physical AI coordination, requiring agents to maintain shared spatiotemporal context. To address these challenges, a network of holonic digital twins (HDT-Nets) framework is proposed to deliver real-time physical AI inference through holonic agents that actively reason about their environment rather than passively mirror physical assets. Each HDT is realized as a hierarchical structure spanning the physical agent and network edge, reasoning autonomously at the local level while cooperating with neighboring HDTs to form collectively intelligent units. In HDT-Net, causal Markov blankets spanning sensing, communication, and control determine which agents must coordinate and enable counterfactual reasoning over multi-domain interventions. Active inference within these boundaries unifies perception, action, and learning by minimizing expected free energy while deciding which beliefs to transmit based on their cognitive value to the receiver. Category theory ensures that transmitted beliefs preserve semantic structure across heterogeneous agents with incompatible representations. Finally, integrated information theory quantifies when collective intelligence exceeds independent operation and how network intelligence evolves through coordinated learning and information exchange.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEM-3，一个概率性全球天气预测模型，通过显式多时间步推理解决固定自回归时间步带来的误差累积与可用性之间的权衡问题，实现单一权重下灵活配置时间步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预测模型依赖预定的固定自回归时间步，存在基本权衡：短时间步能精细分辨日周期内的大气动力学但在给定预测范围内增加误差累积；长时间步减少误差累积但限制高可预测性的短期预测可用性。

Method: 提出GEM-3，一个概率性全球天气模型，采用显式多时间步推理。单一训练权重集可在推理时配置模型时间步，并采用混合时间步训练。模型为轻量级邻域注意力transformer，约134M参数，使用等矩形网格，相比前代GEM-2有多项架构改进。

Result: 混合时间步训练相比时间步专门模型持续改善滚动稳定性。GEM-3在中等范围概率预测技能上接近最先进水平，具有稳定的扩展范围滚动、高效的训练和推理、以及决策相关诊断能力。

Conclusion: GEM-3通过多时间步推理成功平衡了预测性与可用性，构建了一个实用的预测系统，耦合了近乎SOTA的中等范围概率技能、稳定的扩展范围滚动、高效训练推理与决策相关诊断。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [118] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了MS-MLB，这是首个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的多发性硬化症(MS)与健康对照组分类的开放基准测试平台。梯度提升算法在保留集上表现最佳，MS研究得分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化症(MS)的诊断主要依赖临床评估、MRI和实验室检查。虽然血液RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关的免疫信号，但目前缺乏一个标准化、可重复的机器学习基准平台来评估和比较不同的分类算法，且需要严格控制数据泄漏问题。

Method: 构建了MS-MLB基准框架，使用公开的GSE17048队列数据，将其转换为MS与健康对照组分类任务。采用了泄漏控制的共享评估流程，包括嵌套交叉验证、分层保留集、bootstrap置信区间、ROC和精确召回分析、校准测量以及探索性MS研究评分。研究者可以在不重新配置评估的情况下重新运行整个流程。

Result: 在最终基准测试总结中，梯度提升(Gradient Boosting)算法在保留集上排名第一，MS研究得分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，敏感性为0.950，特异性为0.778，F1分数为0.927，Brier分数为0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个专注于基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的MS与健康对照组分类的开放基准，框架内建立了文档化的外部模型提交途径。该评分仅用于研究比较，尚未经过临床验证。基准平台已在GitHub开源发布，为MS血液转录组学机器学习研究提供了标准化的比较平台。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [119] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过挖掘冻结预测器残差的纠正性特征来驱动轻量级后验纠正器，在六个数据集上超越所有专用特征工程系统，将纠正器改进效果翻倍并将最弱骨干误差降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器常以结构化、重复的方式失效，而通过微调修复成本高昂。现有自动化特征工程建模数据生成过程，而纠正性特征应建模模型失效过程。需要一种轻量级方法来发现可解释特征并纠正冻结预测器的系统性错误。

Method: 提出CRAFTER框架，保持骨干网络冻结，使用两个互补生成器挖掘残差：1）原始输入通道的组合搜索；2）大语言模型提议命名特征组合、二元标志和可执行代码。通过验证集门控机制接受或拒绝候选特征，使用验证集选择的纠正器应用接受的特征或保持预测不变。该源无关流水线允许在相同条件下评估不同特征工程系统。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上的实验表明，CRAFTER在每个特征预算下均超越所有专用特征工程系统。纠正器改进效果约翻倍，最弱骨干网络的误差降低达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下保持鲁棒，即使应用于微调骨干网络之上仍然有效。

Conclusion: 纠正性特征发现能有效修复冻结预测器的系统性失效，CRAFTER框架通过组合搜索和LLM协同生成候选特征，配合验证集门控机制，实现了优于传统特征工程方法的性能提升，为特征源归因提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [120] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个概率编程语言，用于量化LLM应用流程中的不确定性，使开发者能够在不增加代码的情况下进行推理扩展实验，并通过定理证明代理案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用大语言模型（LLM）构建可靠应用面临巨大挑战。尽管LLM在各类任务中表现出色，但其输出往往缺乏准确性且无法提供明确的置信度度量。这种不确定性在涉及多次LLM调用和其他工具的复杂流程中进一步累积，导致开发者和终端用户难以信任系统结果。

Method: 提出一种用于编程LLM流程的概率语言。该语言允许开发者量化并传播应用流程中的不确定性，并支持无需额外编写代码即可实验不同的推理扩展技术，从而简化了可靠性管理。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了所提概率语言的量化与不确定性传播能力，并基于Rocq定理证明器构建了一个定理证明代理案例，证明了方法的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该概率语言为构建可靠LLM应用提供了系统化的不确定性管理方案，显著降低了开发复杂度，为提升LLM应用的可信度奠定了基础。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [121] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的计算导向时间序列-语言模型，通过将感知任务（确定性统计计算）与表达任务（LLM生成）解耦，解决了多变量时间序列描述中的自监督陷阱问题，在多变量理解任务上显著超越GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5.4-nano等更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列-语言模型训练存在两个核心问题：1)自监督陷阱——让LLM读取序列并生成描述的方案中，标签质量受限于模型本应学习的感知能力，数据无法提供超出标注者认知范围的信息；2)数据缺陷——多数数据集仅使用单变量，而关键模式（跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）仅在多变量场景下出现，恰好暴露了标注LLM的能力边界。这导致现有方法在可靠性、真实性、可扩展性三者间面临三难困境。

Method: 采用感知与描述解耦策略：确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列计算统计特征集合，LLM仅负责将这些预计算事实语言化。感知任务（LLM表现不佳）由计算处理，表达任务由LLM承担。基于此方法构建CGTime——4B参数的计算导向时间序列-语言模型。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上显著优于更大规模的通用模型：在保留基准测试上取得最佳多变量事实得分（0.283），超越GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和GPT-5.4-nano（0.203），该差距在经Holm校正的配对显著性检验中对所有基线均成立。生成的描述中可验证数值事实准确度更高，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过将感知任务交由确定性计算、表达任务交由LLM的解耦方案，成功突破了时间序列-语言模型的自监督陷阱，在保持可扩展性的同时实现了可靠性与真实性的统一，为多变量时间序列理解提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [122] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与推理显式分离，利用现成感知模型重建结构化3D证据，并通过LoRA微调LLM进行推理，在空间推理基准上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而大语言模型擅长组合与符号推理。这种互补优势促使作者探索将3D感知与推理显式分离的替代范式，而非通过大规模训练联合学习隐式的3D感知与推理。

Method: 提出解耦空间推理器（DiSR）框架：使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据，然后通过LoRA微调LLM仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理。无需大规模3D视觉问答训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上达到竞争性性能，无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是端到端建模的可扩展且有效的替代范式，能够提升可解释性、模块化程度和计算效率。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [123] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了因子化生成模型中一个关键误解：仅匹配风格变量的边缘分布并不能保证其与类别标签独立，即使全局统计量显示完美匹配，类别信息仍可能通过潜在泄漏方式保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型普遍通过将风格潜在变量的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验来声称实现了风格与类别的独立性，但作者质疑这种解释的正确性，指出仅匹配边缘分布并不能约束类别条件分布，导致潜在风格仍然可以高度预测类别标签。

Method: 作者推导了一个精确分解公式，证明这种不匹配是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一；通过案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在基线，在六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程、先验几何、监督）下进行实验验证；采用线性探针恢复类别标签、聚类准确率评估、外部评估类别条件生成等方法；测试了四种缓解策略和事后条件先验方法。

Result: 实验显示模型在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%准确率恢复类别标签（随机水平10%）；模型达到99.15%聚类准确率，但外部评估类别条件生成成功率仅16%；四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变；事后条件先验在MNIST上将外部评估类别生成提升至0.97，但在CIFAR-10上仅0.41，经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布计算的散度无法证明其与类别标签的独立性，单独报告边缘统计量不能验证因子化生成模型中普遍声称的属性，消除边缘分布不匹配是获得预期因子化的必要但非充分条件。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [124] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将对抗流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络，解决了原有框架在不完全信息博弈中的密度无效问题，在性能与速度上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多算法将强化学习与反事实遗憾最小化结合，但在不完全信息博弈中，对生成式采样框架的研究较少。已有生成流网络在完全信息博弈中有效，但在不完全信息博弈中约束不可行，需提出新的框架。

Method: 将对抗流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络。证明原有生成流网络在不完全信息博弈中的约束无法得到有效的密度与训练目标，提出IFlowNets作为AFlowNets的严格推广，实现有效训练。

Result: 在三种标准博弈环境下的初步实验表明，IFlowNets在性能与速度上与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）及标准强化学习方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功地将生成流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈，解决了原有框架的局限性，为不完全信息博弈提供了新的有效生成式采样方法。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [125] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架和PRISM-Eval基准，通过结构化规则监督解决多模态指令遵循中的多规则优先级问题，在仅10K样本下将模型准确率提升20%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态指令通常包含多个重要性不等的要求，而现有训练数据仍将指令遵循简化为回答单一问题，存在显著差距。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架（生成person-task对、前缀引导规则集、质量过滤规则、结构化验证轨迹），并引入PRISM-Eval评估基准，使用Loose和Strict两种无需推理时评判模型的确定性匹配指标。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准性能，且收益迁移至4个开源MLLM（包括dense和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化规则监督是实现多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [126] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DG-FedReuse通过重用过期缓存更新实现83.36-85.42%的上行通信节省，但准确率略有下降（-5.29至-0.14个百分点），且研究明确限定为模拟器层面的机制刻画，未证明实际系统效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临本地优化和模型更新传输的重复开销问题，通信成本高昂。现有方法如Top-K压缩虽然能减少传输量，但仍需每轮传输新鲜更新。研究者希望探索通过重用缓存的历史更新来进一步降低通信开销，同时控制重用带来的潜在性能损失。

Method: 提出DG-FedReuse模拟器层面机制：1) 当随机头部梯度差异代理低于轮次依赖阈值时，允许选定客户端贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新；2) 通过硬缓存年龄限制和新鲜客户端配额约束重用；3) 新鲜更新采用自适应逐张量Top-K数值场表示。实验覆盖六个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性（α=0.5）和三个随机种子。

Result: 在90轮预算下：1) 建模更新数据场上行节省83.36-85.42%，相比匹配Top-K FedAvg的76.88%；2) 种子对齐准确率差异-5.29至-0.14个百分点；3) 最佳测试准确率差异-2.38至+0.45个百分点；4) 对称密集模型下行敏感分析下，标题节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对Top-K FedAvg的增量收益仅3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究明确界定为模拟器层面的机制刻画，未建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛或优于现有过期更新/惰性聚合方法的结论。通信节省结论高度依赖于核算边界，强调了研究范围的重要性，避免过度宣称实际系统效益。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [127] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoThread，一种用于强化学习推理系统线程优化的自适应方法，通过物理信息神经网络预测和队列模型约束，平均加速18.4%，吞吐量提升至现有方法的1.7-1.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真环决策系统中，强化学习推理受到仿真器端执行开销的约束，工作负载高度动态且对运行时线程配置敏感。现有多线程策略难以在执行前或执行期间匹配合适的线程资源，导致资源竞争、调度开销增加和吞吐量降低。

Method: 提出AutoThread混合自适应线程调优方法：(1)采用物理信息神经算子(PINO)作为线程数预测器；(2)引入有限源M/M/1排队模型约束和引导预测；(3)执行负载感知的在线微调以补偿预测误差并优化资源分配。通过任务执行时间与调度时间之比确定最优线程数。

Result: 相比静态策略平均加速18.4%；吞吐量分别达到XGBoost和Reinforcer方法的1.7倍和1.8倍；相比最先进方法执行时间最多减少83.8%。

Conclusion: AutoThread有效缓解了RL推理中的仿真瓶颈问题，通过结合物理信息预测模型和排队理论约束，在动态工作负载下实现了快速准确的线程数估计和资源分配优化。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT提出参数高效的过拟合感知多任务微调框架，通过适配器状态替代全模型检查点，在保持性能的同时大幅降低了存储和部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务监督微调（SFT）中，不同任务在不同时间达到最佳泛化性能，但现有msft方法通过任务级roll-out、exclusion和rollback解决此问题时，需要保存完整模型检查点作为调度器状态，导致阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署方面成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练主干网络，仅训练注入的适配器：1）在任务级峰值时回滚适配器检查点；2）继续在剩余活跃混合上训练；3）在层级别，每个适配器将AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层应用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，保持输入线性、秩有界且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留主干比较协议下，AuroSFT达到61.36%平均准确率，相比msft参考行的59.85%提升1.51个百分点，并在所有五个主干网络上均获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT成功地将过拟合感知多任务SFT的状态载体从完整模型检查点重构为紧凑、可合并的适配器状态，在提升性能的同时实现了参数高效的训练，为多任务学习提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [129] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在北极航运导航中，基于逆强化学习的奖励模型。结果显示非线性共享奖励优于线性基线，而添加潜在上下文反而降低性能。行为差异主要由可观测因素而非隐藏偏好解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能辅助导航有助于北极航运适应快速变化的海冰环境，但可靠部署需要可解释且对环境变化鲁棒的奖励模型。逆强化学习提供了从船舶轨迹恢复奖励的框架，但尚不清楚潜在表示是否恢复真实的隐藏偏好，还是仅仅重新编码了已观测到的状态信息。

Method: 在3,186条AIS航次数据（来自9个北极航运季节的202艘船舶）上进行受控评估，比较了线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励以及基于相同非线性架构的潜在上下文模型。采用行为分析、上下文探测和预注册的特征隐藏消融实验进行验证。

Result: 非线性奖励的留出似然度比线性基线提高50.9%，而添加船舶特定潜在上下文反而使性能降低16.5%。船舶层面的行为差异主要由可观测的航线和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。预测准确率、航线保真度和奖励迁移性给出了不同的模型排名。

Conclusion: 在添加船舶特定潜在上下文之前，应先验证观测到的航线、环境和船舶特征是否已解释行为差异。没有任何单一指标足以评估学习到的奖励。这一发现支持在安全关键领域进行更可信的AI部署。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了AuroOFT方法，在量化正交微调（qOFT）基础上引入零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支，在保持量化兼容性的同时实现非线性校正，在Qwen2.5模型上相比qOFT提升Macro-6指标1.30-2.70%，相比QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，同时节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化正交微调（qOFT）方法仅支持线性正交变换，无法进行输入依赖的非线性校正，限制了其在低比特语言模型参数高效适配中的表现能力。

Method: AuroOFT将qOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支，在每个适配的线性层上附加零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差。该方法将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜在空间，并使用带界限或令牌依赖门控的自适应非线性基。零初始化的上投影使AuroOFT在初始化时与qOFT功能等价，正交性作为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置下，AuroOFT相比匹配的qOFT在Macro-6指标上提升1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，同时在代表性规模上相比QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT成功突破了qOFT的线性变换限制，通过引入非线性残差分支实现了更强大的参数高效适配能力，在性能提升的同时保持了参数效率优势，为低比特语言模型的微调提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [131] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size.
